- **redteam**: Adversarial agent-pair harness: one model writes code (plan → implement), a second reviews it adversarially; the draft PR is the human checkpoint (a single-model test-first TDD mode is also available). ([source](.))
- **redteam**: Repeatable, multi-agent, black-box adversarial testing of any authorized target — a network, host, web app, cloud tenant, or bespoke system. An interactive intake derives the target and its authorized scope; the engagement then follows NIST SP 800-115 with MITRE ATT&CK + NIST SP 800-53/53A mapping, standards-grounded test catalogs (OWASP WSTG/ASVS, CIS, OSSTMM), a human approval gate, and evidence capture.
